# Prior-detection sweep for five TESS transit signals

Generated 2026-10-19T00:53:04Z by `results/prior_search/scripts/build_report.py` from the JSONL query logs in `results/prior_search/log/`. Every statement of absence below is backed by a logged query (section 2).

## 0. Totals

| quantity | value |
|---|---|
| distinct services/sources queried | 32 |
| logged queries (excluding the superseded first VizieR run) | 1585 |
| queries that failed or were unreachable | 155 |
| SPOC TCE rows scanned (132 single+multi-sector tables; all planet numbers) | 341,351 |
| TESS-SPOC FFI DV targets (TCE hosts) scanned via bulk DV scripts | 216,723 |
| ExoFOP TOI rows / CTOI rows (downloaded today) | 8,151 / 5,138 |

| Zenodo records deep-grepped (metadata + file contents) | 157 |
| GitHub repositories cloned and grepped | 101 |
| **automatic alerts checked (SPOC TCE rows + TESS-SPOC DV targets)** | **558,074** |

Services: AAVSO VSX, ASAS-SN Sky Patrol, Crossref, ExoFOP-TESS, GitHub, Google Scholar, Harvard Dataverse, MAST HLSP products, MAST Observations, MAST SPOC DV products, MAST SPOC TCE tables, MAST TESS-SPOC DV products, MAST TIC, MIT TESS TOI releases, NASA ADS, NASA Exoplanet Archive TAP, OpenAlex, SIMBAD, Semantic Scholar, T16 catalog (ApJS MRT), VizieR, VizieR (Gaia DR3), Web search engine, ZTF/IRSA, Zenodo, Zooniverse/Planet Hunters Talk, arXiv, figshare, ijspeert+2024, kostov+2025, note, project known_signals.csv

Section order: 0 totals, 1 verdicts, 3 identifiers, 4 neighbour hits, 5 failures and gaps, then 2 (the full query log, ~1,600 rows) as the final appendix. Rows from superseded streams (first VizieR run with a false period-column match; first TESS-SPOC script run that got 404s; first Zenodo discovery run that got HTTP 400 for size=100; first VSX run that used a 1-degree radius) are kept in the log, tagged (superseded), and excluded from the totals.

## 1. Summary table (verdicts)

Period matching convention: a catalogued period counts as a match if it equals P, 2P, P/2, 3P or P/3 within 1 %. "Prior detection" means any public record of a transit-like or periodic signal on the target TIC, or on any TIC/Gaia source within 60 arcsec, at a matching period, dated before this project's first public deposit (GitHub fergmolina/tess-transit-candidates, 2026-10-17 23:01 UTC; Zenodo 10.5281/zenodo.23443245, 2026-10-18). The author's own deposits (also Zenodo 23445705 and 23460915, 2026-10-18/19) were found by the Zenodo, OpenAlex, GitHub and web-search layers and are listed for completeness; they are not prior reports by others. Three classes of record were distinguished: (a) refereed literature and official catalogs (TOI/CTOI/TCE); (b) public, timestamped but non-refereed candidate lists (GitHub/Zenodo deposits by independent searchers); (c) incidental catalog rows (a star listed in a survey input table or with an unrelated variability class).

**Reading of the eumemic/exoplanets record.** Repository created 2026-10-07 21:48 UTC by a pseudonymous author ("eumemic"; site credits "Aska Digital"), 30 commits, last push 2026-10-18 23:31 UTC, built with Claude Code and explicitly modelled on Rabtsevich's TIC 4206066 search; it covers 9,979 nearby K/M dwarfs (S97-S105 plus all earlier sectors), 6,430 TOI hosts, and a full-sky search of 4.04 million FGKM dwarfs (2,990 rows in candidates_allsky.csv, 645 "counted" candidates). It is a public, git-timestamped candidate list, not a refereed publication. It is NOT indexed by general web search for these TIC numbers (only one generic query surfaced the repository); GitHub code search does index it. Its README cites other October-2026 Zenodo deposits by independent searchers (Tovar Contreras 10.5281/zenodo.23134068, Ozturk 23118499, Fraser 23249265, Chopra 23257784); all four were downloaded and grepped (pdftotext for PDFs) and contain none of the five TICs.

**Statement for a reviewer on object 2.** TOI-2514.01 at 6.3951 d was found (ExoFOP TOI table, NASA Exoplanet Archive toi table, ExoFOP target page, Howard 2022) and excluded; no TOI, CTOI, TCE, Exoplanet Archive, VizieR, literature or follow-up record on TIC 317134140 or any neighbour lists a period matching 0.5719, 1.1437 or 0.2859 d; the only prior mention of the 0.5719-d signal is the eumemic/exoplanets repository (first commit 2026-10-08 03:14 UTC).

**Ecosystem note.** The sweep found at least six independent, Rabtsevich-style amateur TESS candidate deposits made in October 2026 (eumemic, ahrazzle mirror, Zarco, Rahle, Chopra, Fraser, Tovar Contreras, Ozturk; plus 99 TESS-candidate repositories cloned and grepped). Any future "no prior report" statement for a TESS signal must therefore include a GitHub code search and a Zenodo file-content grep, not only ExoFOP/ADS/VizieR.

Not covered / limitations (explicit):
- TESS-SPOC DV (FFI TCE) products exist only for Sectors 36-85 and the S56-S69 multi-sector run; there is no TESS-SPOC alert coverage for S1-35 or S86+. Sectors of our detections outside that range: TIC 89247735 S13, S93, S104 (its S66 is inside: no TESS-SPOC TCE there); TIC 317134140 S6, S32-33 (early), S87, S98, S107; TIC 80387959 S90+ ; TIC 120444365 S2, S29, S96; TIC [withheld] S10, S90+. QLP has no public TCE list at all.
- GitHub code search has index lag: the exact phrase "TIC 89247735" returns 0 results although two public repositories (ours and Zarco's) contain it; absence in code search is weak evidence. The clone-and-grep of 101 candidate repositories (section 2, streams s8/s11/s15) is the real check.
- ExoFOP: the 2 observing notes attached to TOI-2514 are visible only to logged-in users and were not read; the LCO notes file and all observation records were read.
- Semantic Scholar: HTTP 429 on every call in both passes (fast and 12-s spaced); not covered.
- The VizieR catalog id used for "SuperWASP periodic variables" (J/A+A/615/A20) did not resolve to a SuperWASP table; SuperWASP/WASP is covered only through the all-catalog cone (no WASP table within 60 arcsec of any target) and the HATSouth/WASP/KELT discovery route via ExoFOP and the Exoplanet Archive.
- Zenodo record 22967456 (Rabtsevich, TIC 4206066) file contents were not grepped (download aborted after 20 min); its metadata contains none of the five TICs.
- Internet Archive (Wayback CDX) was offline during the sweep, so no third-party snapshot of the eumemic repository could be obtained; priority rests on GitHub server-side push-event, release and fork timestamps (section 2, s? 'GitHub server-side timestamps').
- NASA ADS full-text search: no API token on this machine and the web UI is a JavaScript shell; NOT performed. Substitutes: SIMBAD bibliography (CDS object-literature link; 0 references for all five stars), arXiv API (metadata), Semantic Scholar, OpenAlex, Crossref, Google Scholar (returned "did not match" but with a service-error banner, so treated as partial).
- arXiv full text: the arXiv API indexes title/abstract/author/comment fields only. Full text was covered for the specific catalog papers by downloading their source tarballs and the T16 machine-readable tables.
- QLP: MIT publishes no public QLP TCE list; QLP-origin candidates reach the public only as TOIs/CTOIs (covered). tess.mit.edu has an expired TLS certificate; fetched with verification disabled.
- Planet Hunters TESS Talk: the Zooniverse Talk search API result is logged as returned; the PHT candidate pipeline posts its candidates as CTOIs on ExoFOP (covered).
- ZTF: four targets are below the ZTF southern limit; TIC 317134140 (Dec -17) has no ZTF light curve at IRSA.
- Social media (X/Twitter, Reddit, Cloudy Nights): only via the web search engine, which does not honour site operators reliably; no mention found.
- HATSouth and NGTS have no public candidate lists; their discoveries are covered through the NASA Exoplanet Archive and ExoFOP.
- Zenodo: the search API indexes metadata only, so every recent (since 2026-08-01) TESS/transit/TIC-matching record's files were downloaded and grepped (section 2, stream s10/s12); records outside those query terms could be missed.
- GitHub: code search covers only indexed repositories (and the gh code-search endpoint requires the search index); all repositories found by repository search with TESS/transit keywords were cloned and grepped.
